<image-desc>
A magic circle drawn in ink. The diagram consists of a square frame containing two concentric circles. 

In the four corners of the square, outside the circle, are written the names of four demons:
- Top Left: Belial
- Top Right: Satan
- Bottom Left: Lucifer
- Bottom Right: Belzebub

Along the four cardinal axes, outside the circle but within the square, are the directions:
- Top: Oriens
- Bottom: Occidens
- Left (written vertically): Septẽtrio
- Right (written vertically): Meridies

Within the band between the two concentric circles, four names are written:
- Left side: Apolyn, Beruthinus
- Right side: Aendor, Havalot

In the center of the inner circle, the following text is written:
"Nome[n] magist[ri] de tali ordi-
ne. Nome[n] discipuli"
</image-desc>

**C**um volueris habere amorem aliquamque
mulieris via sive longinqua fuerit
qumque tam nobili quam paupere in quacumque
die ut nocte via sive in augmento sive in
amicitie detrimento primo debes habere
una quandam columbam totam albam et cartam
factam de cane feta dum est in amore
de qua terra levissimum et debet scire
quod predicta carta potentissima est ad amorem
mulierum secundum dominum. Debes etiam habere ca-
lamum aquile et in loco occulto accipe
dictam columbam et cum dentibus morde eam
venas cor ita ut cor egrediatur. et cum
calamo aquile in dicta carta cum dicto sanguine
scribe nomen illius quam vis secundum talem ymaginem
mulieris nude quam magis fas dicendo formam
talem. **N.** filiam talis quod habere desidero
inter sex spirituum calidorum videlicet **tubal. sa-
than. fauces. cupido. asalion. duliaco.**
quod ipsa me diligat super omnes viventes ipsius
mundi. qua facta scribe in fronte nomen eius et
hoc nomen **tubal** dicendo tu es talis fi-
lia talis detenta ad meam voluntatem disposi-
ta et tu es **tubal** in fronte eius retineo
amanans ligando sensum eius capitis sui
ad me immo cupientem postea adhuc
scribe in brachio suo dextro **sathan** et in

<image-desc>
A magical diagram featuring four concentric circles with a large five-pointed star (pentagram) in the center. A vertical sword (ensis) is drawn with its hilt at the top and its point touching the top of the pentagram. 

The cardinal directions are labeled outside the circles:
- Top (at the sword hilt): oriens
- Right: Meridies
- Bottom: occidens
- Left: Septentrion / ut Aquilon

Inside the rings, there are sixteen names arranged in four radial groups:
- Upper-Left: Dyma, Dema, Lam, Aa
- Upper-Right: Loy, Fin, Sine, Tan
- Lower-Right: zymofos, Rodobavp, tantrop, locobow
- Lower-Left: Panbar, Abelucabel, vrmasow, fivama

Additionally, there are four names written along the bottom arc of the rings: catomofon, tautaban, tam a fin, ganon. Decorative hooks extend from the diagonal axes of the outermost circle.
</image-desc>

<image-desc>A magical circle or diagram consisting of two concentric rings. Inside the central circle is the text "Locus magistri" (The Master's Place). In the space between the two rings, various words and names are written in a circular orientation, including "salay", "sumotores", "teme", "Bemo", "Zemo", "Gutvoy", "Zitou", and "Amigzi". Above the "Locus magistri" text within the inner ring is the word "onoy". Outside the perimeter of the outer circle, additional labels are written such as ".vsyw.", ".salaul.", and ".Elitary.".</image-desc>

<meta>The text consists of a formal conjuration of spirits/demons using divine names and titles of saints. The diagram is likely a protective circle for the practitioner to stand in during the ritual.</meta>

<image-desc>
A complex circular magical diagram or "circle of power". The diagram features an outer circle surrounding a square. Inside the square is a central circle labeled "Locus magri" (Locus magistri - The Master's Place). 

The four rectangular sections between the central circle and the square's edges are labeled with the cardinal directions:
- Top: "ori ens" (Oriens - East)
- Right: "meri dies" (Meridies - South)
- Bottom: "occi dens" (Occidens - West)
- Left: "sep tētrio" (Septentrio - North)

Outside the square, within the outer circle, there are various symbols and names:
- At the top: "Lantraych"
- At the right: "ferēmni"
- At the bottom: A sequence of occult or magical characters resembling a mix of pseudo-Hebrew and planetary sigils.
- On the left: A geomantic figure (four dots in a column) and a grid-like symbol.
- On the top right: A linear figure consisting of two intersecting lines with circles at the terminal points.
</image-desc>

<header>Circulus</header>

<image-desc>A large magic circle consisting of two concentric rings. Inside the inner ring, lines divide the space into segments. Two triangles are inscribed within the circle, one pointing upward and one downward. Each triangle contains a pentagram (five-pointed star) with the syllable "on" written at each of its five points. Along the inner circumference of the upper part of the circle is written "hic sedeat qui sup sigm". Along the lower circumference is written "hic sedeat magister sup sigm alatave sinistro".</image-desc>

diem Ad effectum perveniat sed nunquam perveniet
per se seipsos decipiunt et opera sua amittunt
et malignis viribus unice verbi ipsorum quando
quando amunt et. Unde dicit malihus sciencia
huius secularis potentia. sic ipsa sciencia pualis
et ab aliis quia omnia phantasmata exprimata sunt
corrupta ideo quidam sunt fictilia et sic omnia
percepta tenent. igitur diligenter prenotatis
ad nostrum propositum redeamus et videamus
cui et quo exornatus sive operator esse debe-
at et quod ei sit utile ad operandum ne fallatur
<margin>#</margin> In primis unusquisque hanc artem facturam ad quod
vocatur liber preservatos ab omni pollutione
mentis et corporis se debet abstinere a cibo
potu et verbis otiosis sive immoderatis
et sit mundis indutis vestibus nova die
hanc ante opus inceptum debet audire
in qualibet missam unam et librum istum sacrum
deferre et ponere super altari donec missa
finiatur quolibet die quousque in pacti fuerint omnes
et hoc devotissime faciat cum orationibus
et ieiuniis quia sicut scriptum est per omnia
omnes occultos improborum celari non
revelatur quia divini sacramenti secreta non
pandunt Et cottidie post missam librum
portabis domum et habeas locum secretum

# Volach
magnus praeses et apparet sicut puer habens alas
admodum angeli equitat super draco-
nem duo habens capita dat ad plenitudinem
veras responsiones de occultis thesauris ubi vi-
dentur et apparet serpentibus et absque illis custo-
dibus posse omnem serpentem tradit in manus
exorcistae habet sub se xxvii legiones

---

# Alaneuon
dux est fortis et potens
et apparet in similitudine pulcherrima inter
ductili coronati corva cantat super annu-
lum dat ad plenitudinem veras responsiones de praeteri-
tibus praesentibus et futuris et de occultis the-
sauris in quibus locis videntur et apparet
dat gratiam amorem mulierum et praecipue
puellarum habet sub se xxvii legiones

---

# Vuerrías
marchio magnus et fortis
regnans et imperans in Affrica in-
tribuit docet ad plenitudinem grammaticam
logicam et rhetoricam de heretate et fa-
cit loca thesaurorum flumina et occulta
ostendere facit etiam hominem veloci-
ssimo cursu mare et flumina transire ut
etiam sine metu in specie militis apparet
equitat super equum nigrum et egregium
habet sub se xxx vii legiones

---

# Aim
praeses magnus et apparet in flamma
ignea sed cum suscepit formam humanam

<vocab>Volach, Alaneuon, Vuerrías, Aim, praeses, dux, marchio, legiones, thesauris, exorcista, grammaticam, logicam, rhetoricam</vocab>

<image-desc>A large magic circle diagram occupies the top half of the page. It consists of three concentric rings containing Latin inscriptions. Outside the circle, at the four cardinal points, are labels and divine names: 'Meridies' (South) with 'Sabaoth' at the top; 'Occidens' (West) with 'Amadumel' at the right; 'Septentrio' (North) with 'Adonay' at the bottom; and 'Oriens' (East) with 'Eloha' at the left. The text within the rings is repetitive, beginning with 'Hunc circulu facio in noie dei...' (I make this circle in the name of God...) followed by the persons of the Trinity. Cross symbols '♁' serve as delimiters within the rings. To the far left, partially cut off, additional text reads 'In noie p(at)ris et filii et sp(irit)us s(an)c(t)i...'.</image-desc>

->$\maltese$ Regmū dūp<-

<image-desc>A circular protective talisman or magic circle. The diagram consists of three concentric rings of Latin text surrounding a central quartered circle. 

The outermost ring contains blessings and biblical invocations, including "Benedictio dñi noster Ihu Xpi" (Blessing of our Lord Jesus Christ), "benedictio s'ae marie matris dñi" (blessing of Saint Mary the mother of the Lord), and "benedictio oĩa opera dñi dñm bñdicite celi" (bless the Lord all ye works of the Lord). 

The three rings each begin with a ritual formula: the outer ring states "Hūc circulu facio i noie patris" (I make this circle in the name of the Father), the middle ring "Hūc circulu facio in noie filij" (in the name of the Son), and the inner ring "Hūc circulu facio in noie spūs scī" (in the name of the Holy Spirit). 

Much of the circular text repeats the verse from Psalm 117/118:16: "dextera dñi fecit virtutē dextera dñi exaltavit me" (The right hand of the Lord hath wrought strength: the right hand of the Lord hath exalted me). 

In the center, a circle is divided by a cross into four quadrants. Each quadrant contains the word "deus" (God). Four additional words or names of power are written across the center: "fien", "apion", "on", and "H·P".</image-desc>

<vocab>benedictio, dextera domini, Ihesus Christus, Maria, trinitas, magic circle, experimentum, talisman, protection charm, liturgical Latin, paleography</vocab>

> und ghelidre dussen schaden ghelosen kan
> sonder der man der de sonder an d' hoch-
> stame cruce geston dat was unse liebe her
> Ihesus cristi der eyn schepper uber hymell
> und erdrich ist der buse dussen schaden
> disse ankunfft disse kranckheit dut gesche
> und ghelidre dat helff mir der vater
> der son und der hillig geist und helffe
> euch und mir alle dy obenende tzit
> dy uns god yn d' ewige lebe sint dar
> wort war In d' name des vaters des
> sones und des hilligen geist Amen

---

hat eyn mensche den worm so mach
man auch spreche dar ge hat menich
mensche gholffen das bewert ist

# Item vor alle fule elend machet man disse
## machtige salbe

so sal man neme’ eyn kruid gehit ayeten
dat sal ma’ breche’ uff eyne frytag vor
mittage unde ey iglich mensche sal III sijn
glaube sprechen eer’ man brechen wille
und so endang. ouch dat kruid yn de’ mey
meist elend es nu recht ge’use hat
so sal ma’ dat kruid dy blattere abe strijffen
und dy kleyne geyken und dan yn eyner
morsere stossen sere kleyne und dy cruise
yn syn hende bynahe uß dragede angericht
so sal ma’ wine neme und yn eyner
pannen lassen <unclear>sunckers</unclear> <unclear>vembart</unclear> smalts
und dar under eyn deel wein meng’ und doch
dat es nicht vere sette werde tie man dat
schale dordren wegebraid samm’ oda’
gestossen dat ist ußernassen gud ayen guit
ouch dy wond’ allzeit wasche mit wassen
an de’ schate sy uff songe egede’ lo’
boy ouch ab’ der been recht locher haete
so en ist besers nicht dan dat ma’ nymet
nicht eine boffel allene adden dry eymer vel
wassers und mache daruff lange d’ ran
stiften dy da’ doret sy von eymeche holtze
und von haselnotten holtze <unclear>al</unclear> ma’ dy
lange gemachet hat so sal ma’ an beiden
syden duken met eyn galle mettijn follen

<vocab>salbe, kruid, wegebraid, smalt, holtze, been, morsere</vocab>

### Item von der vngestalte pocke vnder de ougē
### als ma es nennt gorssia

### Item eyn ander gud luttert water dar ayan sal
neme dy rode snecken vnd sal se thū yn eyne
quarte dyppe mit dē galbvull vnd darzn
ruffē glene adē dry löffelvoll saltes p
vnd dar setzen yn dē ovene ij adē drey vhrē
daruf dūnt smals dā sall mā als sygen dūch
eyn dūch vnd yn eyne gotteroff thū vnd
yn dy sūne offset etlich dag so temperire ge
sach solich sal mā vnder dy ougē smere andy
antlitze adē wo ssy not ist Wā dÿ vngesthalt
so sal mā es drey waschē mit gebrante wyn

### Item ander gud proyke dar zu So sal man
neme <unclear>eal osad</unclear> eyer dutery von x adē
xij eyern vnd sal neme thymmīth vnd
eyne löffelvoll saltes vnd das yn eynē
morser stoffen vnd dūch eyn duch wryngē darvnd
So nym eynhals quentīn poluer vō realit
vnd nemy puluer vō getodt quecksilber das
das sal mā alles nū same temperen vnd
wenn man yn eyne bade sytzt So sal mā sich
an dē <unclear>fode</unclear> darmid smere so halit es ayan
sal auch dy pock verhöre uffthz so gefft es

Is eynen fräue dy lende wege thün
So sal man neme leberwurtz ein lot
Und sal es sieden an drey stoffe, und
man sal neme gar ii den stoffe fonff
leffelvoll wynes und sal den myt ey seig
so sal man den machē eyn seynbar
von synderstey drey tage an ey any abēt
Wann man naü bette gehen will so sall
man allegit dy fonff leffelvoll wynes
naü iij dem male dringhē so vergehet
dy kranckheit vor em.

---

Is eyne dranck widder dē ungelucke yn libe
als eyn mensche geslay oder schalt geschossen
und dat ungelucke by em hette up von d' liff
adder wer gebathet fett und fleisch
So sal man neme eyn kruid gehet ajuson

wasser dat sal eyn mensche dringhē so
vertribit et em gronde platcz

seybelhusz raser raser und uff d' erd'
honc ding als gras und hat bletter bijnach
als palcya thun dat selbe.

Iste liber debet fieri de bona materia quousq missa
finiatur et hac deuotissime faciat cum deuotione et
ieiunio sicut scriptum est. Ieiunia et oratio multa
misteria celestia reuelat et archana diuina facienda
paret et quotidie post missam portat librum domum
et habeat locum secretum ab omnibus remotum in quo
maneat nudus nec aliquis sit præsens qui opus est.
Aspergat locum aqua benedicta sed ubi ponat librum suum
cingulo preciose et stola benedicta ligat librum duodecim
nodis circumquaque et flexis genibus ponat librum ipsum
vnde dicat vii psalmos cum oratione longa et
letania faciat et benedicat et consecret istum librum
suum istis verbis designatis cum omni devotione ac
tota cordis affectu et dans operi sua veritate ac
uia sciat que liber benedicat et consecrent
suis scriptis verbis et inscriptis ut uirtute quam
obtinere debeat viz ad sperandum vinculatum propru
valeat ad omnes iuocationes et coniurationes ipsorum
et ad omnia alia experimenta Sed hic quicumque
polluit hunc librum sciat se nihil hic valere
nisi absque vicio omnino perseveret tunc oratione et
letania viuificat librum scriptum et dicat per
orationem humili corde et anima devota tunc dicitur psalmus

> Deus meus miserere mei et propter malitias meas faciam
> oram meam quam percam per meum abnegationem
> et perfecte intulisti Exaudi deus orationem meam famuli
> tui ut in ista die invocantem te valeat et
> audi me sicut exaudisti mariam matrem tuam su//
> spipe dñe humore precem meam exaudi dñe
> uote depeat preces meas et oratio beatissime
> virginis matris mariæ matris tuæ atque omnium sanctorum
> et oratio et facies mea adiuvetur tue pietate
> quas ego peccator iuxta operationem effundo coram te
> in hac hora p[...]tua pessima hora quæ in hac libro
> glinent per precem et graciam tuam ad omnia voluerit
> prestante dno nro ihu xpo qui vivis et regnas [...]

<vocab>liber, missa, ieiunium, oratio, aqua benedicta, VII psalmos, letania, Maria, Jesus Christus, consecratio</vocab>

oramus et deprecamur ne despicias ad <unclear>admunda</unclear> et devocia-
bilia peccata mea sed sicut condonasti peccatoribus miseri-
cordia et pietas tua exaudiat vocem meam famulum tuum
ut liceat in diebus exaudi me digneris clamare ad te
per hoc coniuratio tuis sanctissimo nomine insignito
ut virtute qua obtinere debeat potestatem vincat
sed coerceas potestatem et inseparabiles principes spiritu[um]
etiam de potestate eorum mirabiliter astringat
ut velint ut nolint secundum voluntatem obedire
et cum voluerit eos deus deum congregat
et cum voluerit eos dissipit Oro et scias deum per sanctis-
simum nomen suum quod scribitur quatuor litteris
scilicet ioth vau hee heth quo audito mare retro-
graditur aer commutatur et turbatur terra tremit licet
ignis extinguitur omnia elementa tremunt et pavent
tremunt turbantur per hoc sanctissima nomina tua
on alpha et omega principium et finis el ely sother
eloy elyon sother emanuel sabaoth adonaij
egye ye ye va va exaudiat hoc coniuratio
per sancte illo cui est laus honor et gloria per
infinita secula seculorum amen.

->RUBRICA<-

Si autem aliquis aliud coniuratio experire voluerit
quod non pertinet ad deprecationem suam tunc debet dicere
quatuor vices exaudi me digneris clamare ad te
ut hoc coniuratio virtute obtineat quia obtinere
debet per sanctissimum nomen suum quod scribitur
iiiij litteras scilicet ioth vau hee heth

->Oro<-

Deus mundi totius conditor et redemptor qui celu[m]
super altitudinem nubium extendisti et terra[m] in sta-
bilitate sua super aquas fundasti et mare finib[us]
que pertransire non potest statuisti et solem et lunam
ac stellas in caelo collocasti qui ita in sapientia
fecisti qui vj die hominem ad imaginem ac simi-
litudine tua formasti et ei spiritu[m] vite inspirasti
